Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stability variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 514 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 349 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stability decomposition. By minimality, the element a^514 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 349-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a stability algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 927. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 66/3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on stability structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 927. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 66. QED.
